# Skills · I · part 84

823 entries, interview to interview-kit. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **interview**: Prepare the user for a real, scheduled interview on a tracked application — build a stage-specific prep pack from the archived JD, the actually-submitted resume, and prior-stage feedback, plus cached company research; optionally run a mock interview. Use for "帮我准备面试", "interview prep", "prep me for X", "/interview &lt;company&gt;", or when a tracker entry moves to interviewing. ([Sma1lboy/coforce-apply](https://github.com/Sma1lboy/coforce-apply/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/interview) · ★5)
- **interview**: 질문으로 요구사항을 캐내 /prd 정제용 수집 씨앗(docs/prd/&lt;slug&gt;.md)을 만든다. 빈 템플릿을 혼자 채우는 대신 필요한 질문을 순서에 맞게 묻고, 애매하면 AI가 판단하지 말고 되묻고, 답을 무비판적으로 긍정하지 않고 중립·냉정하게 압박하며, 이번 스택·제약에서 구현이 현실적으로 가능한지도 값싸게 감별한다(깊은 기술 조사는 /plan 몫). 사용자가 "인터뷰", "요구사항 캐줘", "질문으로 요구사항 정리", "/interview ..."라고 할 때 사용한다. 수집 씨앗은 /prd가 정제하며, PRD 생성·검토는 /prd의 범위다. ([taewooyo/tram](https://github.com/taewooyo/tram/tree/HEAD/build/claude/skills/interview) · ★5 · 3 copies)
- **interview**: 以资深前端面试官视角，对前端面试题进行深入、专业的解答，生成结构化的面试准备材料。 ([wkrwez/ai_fullstuck_lt](https://github.com/wkrwez/ai_fullstuck_lt/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/interview) · ★5)
- **interview**: Interview the user about a plan, a design, or a decision until each open decision is settled, before any plan or code. Use when the user says "grill me", "interview me", "stress-test this", or "poke holes in this", or asks if a plan is ready. Not for a clear task that is ready to do, and not for a session where no user can answer. ([andymai/skills](https://github.com/andymai/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/interview) · ★4)
- **interview**: Interview the owner to find out what they want, and why, before designing new work. Use when the main session starts new work or changes an approach the owner picked, even when an issue already describes it. Not for fix rounds, quick clear tasks, or reviewing a written design (use spec-review). ([anthony-spruyt/xfg](https://github.com/anthony-spruyt/xfg/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/interview) · ★4 · 11 copies)
- **interview**: Interview the user to turn a career memory into a vetted story file in their career corpus. - **interview**: Deep project interview that produces actionable specs before implementation begins. Use when the user wants to scope a project, plan a feature, write a spec, stress-test a design, or says "interview" or "grill me". ([galsapir/skills](https://github.com/galsapir/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/interview) · ★4)

USE WHEN /interview, resume interview, context check-in, telos check-in, what's stale, stale data, freshness check, update curr… ([jSydorowicz21/Universal-AI-Infrustructure](https://github.com/jSydorowicz21/Universal-AI-Infrustructure/tree/HEAD/LifeOS/install/skills/Interview) · ★4)
